# Ibex 总实验运行表

本文件记录从 2026-08-23 起提交到 Ibex 的**每一个进程**。方法级汇总结论仍写入 `docs/experiment_log.md`；本表是一行一个 scheduler job 的运行证据。历史本地实验不补造 Ibex 设备信息。

## 强制登记规则

1. job 提交后立即新增一行，状态写 `QUEUED`；拿到实际开始时间、节点和 GPU 后更新同一行。
2. 完成后把“运行状态与主要结果”更新为 `COMPLETED`、`FAILED`、`TIMEOUT`、`CANCELLED` 或 `INVALID`，并给出主指标或失败原因。
3. “支持/反对的结论”必须写成可证伪句子；失败 job 也必须说明它不能支持什么。
4. 实验版本必须符合 `mainExp_[name]_x.y`、`Verify_[name]_x.y`、`Ablation_[name]_x.y` 或 `Other_[name]_x.y`。
5. 开始时间使用 ISO 8601 并包含时区，例如 `2026-08-23T14:30:00+03:00`。
6. 设备至少记录 Ibex GPU 型号和数量；开始后补实际 node。job array 可登记 array ID，但输出中必须能追踪每个 child task。
7. config、git commit、stdout/stderr 和 output 路径缺一不可。重提交产生新 job ID，不覆盖旧行。

## 2026-09-02 Task1/Task2/Task3 固定 pathline 参数联合搜索

本链保持当前任务级方法不变：Task1 为 `fmt_all+kin4→PCA8` 对 Raw-PCA2，
Task2 为 `fmt_all+kin4 + l512_l64_b1e-6` same-VAE，Task3 为
`aivd1w3_dft` 对同维 Raw-PCA residual，并为每套 pathline 参数重训其自己的
Raw/Raw-wide backbone。只改变固定的 `dt_scale / integration_steps /
sampled_steps`；七套配置在全部10个数据条目上分别运行，不混合尺度。开发选择只打开
ordinals 0–7；8–9 在独立选择审计通过前保持关闭。

## 2026-09-02 更强 baseline、FMT 组件消融与噪声鲁棒性

三组实验使用部署源码包 SHA-256 `9f06aaae…abee5`；本地与 Ibex 均通过 Python
编译、4项新增单元测试，Ibex 另通过3个 Bash 脚本语法检查。提交时账号队列为空。
更强 baseline、组件消融和噪声测试互不依赖；各自只有在全部逐次结果完成后才能汇总，
汇总后由不导入正式 summarizer 的独立审计器重算。组件消融和噪声实验的 Task3 临时
checkpoint 只在独立审计 `PASS` 后删除。

## 2026-09-02 Task4-b channel→TBL 跨流场四分类

`mainExp_Task4B_ChannelToTBL_2.3` 固定使用 channel 的 20,641 条 curated
primitive 作为训练集，不设 source holdout；四个模型必须连续三轮达到训练集 0 错误和
正的最小 true-logit margin，才允许各自对同一份 TBL 测试 population 评估一次。
TBL population 为 `p80.5 candidate OR VortexId>0`，因此是人工 hairpin support
增强的条件四分类测试，不是端到端 hairpin detection。部署前已核验 311 个文件，
部署 manifest SHA-256 为 `6a2c93a0f4e10b962f20b87a70a57d01404f2ff85fb1ec79d3b3b9b5f3e87f02`，

## 2026-09-03 更强 baseline 的 pathline 扰动鲁棒性 1.2

用户要求对流场加不同等级噪声，比较我们的方法、旧 baseline 与更强 baseline 的性能下降。1.1 已给出 FMT 与旧 Raw；1.2 在**同一批扰动实现**上补更强 baseline：Task1 普通 DFT 与曲率/扭率/速度序列；Task2 Raw-PCA189、Raw 专调 VAE、普通 DFT（同一 VAE）；Task3 冻结 Raw 与 Raw-wide 网络。所有表示、PCA 宽度和架构从 development 冻结清单读取，运行器启动时核验，不做任何新选择。1.1 的 FMT/Raw 行不重跑，summary 按 1.1 审计哈希核验后并入。部署包 SHA-256 `bebd868a5863455063ae7af6f617653c… 1dab`（6 文件），远端 Audit 脚本旧版备份于 `tmp_uniform_patch/backup_before_noise1p2_*/`；本地与 Ibex 均通过编译、Bash 语法检查，本地用合成行 + 真实 1.1 行做了 summary→audit 全链路自检（audit 在合成 clean 复放处按预期报错）。launcher 写入 `outputs/task123_noise_1p2_submission.tsv` 防重复。

## 2026-09-04 噪声类型扫描（预注册）：Verify_Task123_NoiseTypes_1.1 与 NoiseTypesStrong_1.1

用户要求多试噪声类型与强度，看是否存在能体现 FMT 鲁棒性的扰动。为避免挑选结果，**一次性预注册 22 个扰动条件、全部 13 个臂，结果无论好坏全部报告**。新增 6 类结构化扰动（均在 `FMT_Utils/RobustnessFeatures_3D.py::corrupt_pathline_primitives_3d`，单元测试核验各自应保持的几何量）：时间平滑高斯（5 点滑动平均后归一到 σ）、共模噪声（每时刻 7 条线同一偏移）、邻居种子常量偏移、绕种子点随机轴刚体旋转（15°/45°/90°）、脉冲离群点（2%/5%/10% 采样点、跳幅 σ=1.0 邻居间距）、单调随机时间弯曲（10%/25%/50%）；并把高斯白噪声补到 σ=.005/.01/.02/.03。A 组用 1.1 运行器跑 FMT/Raw，B 组用 1.2 运行器跑更强 baseline，两组同 `corruption_seed`、同种子，扰动实现逐位相同；B 的 summary 等待 A 的独立审计 PASS 后合并。本地 10 项单元测试、Ibex 同一测试与 Bash 语法检查通过；登录节点预检在 tangaroa confirmation 切片 0 上跑完全部 23 条件（45 秒），FMT/几何/普通 DFT 特征全部有限。

**预检给出的无标签诊断**（同一记录、特征相对变化 ‖f′−f‖/‖f‖）：FMT 对刚体旋转和邻居种子偏移**严格不变**（0.000，按构造），普通 DFT 在 90° 旋转下变化 113%；反过来高斯白噪声 σ=.005 就让 FMT 特征变化 54%、σ=.01 达 108%，而普通 DFT 只变 0.1%；共模噪声 σ=.05 下 FMT 变 43%、普通 DFT 0.1%；时间弯曲 10% 下 FMT 变 123%、普通 DFT 2%。这说明 FMT 的无量纲不变量（余弦、手性三重积等）在位移信号很小时被微小噪声翻转；预期 FMT 只在刚体旋转、邻居偏移两类扰动下占优，对其余各类白噪声型扰动仍脆弱。该预期在结果出来前记录于此。

## 2026-09-10 Task6/7/8 independent audit parallel execution

Training and scientific metric definitions remain frozen. The audit uses all particle pairs with an equivalent SciPy Euclidean-distance kernel, checked against the original implementation to 1e-11 on multiple numerical cases and by full 102-record/51-record smoke replays locally and on Ibex. Audit commit: `ea1054f9af40e68ab318c7e96d02406c535f56d9`. Training commits remain `45eefee828f5439f6aad6940b01aafe7803aa691` (HanSampling) and `d9d04b41f0b01af0a2236707a369e1bb2d58cbeb` (VectorFMT).

| Experiment | Phase | Job | Submitted (+03) | Expected device | Dependency |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Verify_Task678_HanSampling_1.1 | audit_dataset | 51703005 | 2026-09-10T14:43:15.879126+03:00 | CPU; 4 cores; no GPU | afterok:51701046 |
| Verify_Task678_HanSampling_1.1 | audit_collect | 51703006 | 2026-09-10T14:43:15.946435+03:00 | CPU; 4 cores; no GPU | afterok:51703005 |
| Verify_Task678_VectorFMT_1.1 | audit_dataset | 51703007 | 2026-09-10T14:43:16.262206+03:00 | CPU; 4 cores; no GPU | afterok:51701310:51701311 |
| Verify_Task678_VectorFMT_1.1 | audit_collect | 51703008 | 2026-09-10T14:43:16.328844+03:00 | CPU; 4 cores; no GPU | afterok:51703007 |

Each dataset audit uses 48 GB and a 2-hour limit; collection uses 8 GB and 20 minutes. Nine dataset audits can run concurrently. Commands and config SHA256 values are saved in each experiment’s `parallel_audit_submissions.jsonl`; execution changes and kernel SHA256 are saved in `audit_execution_change.json`.

The pending serial audits `51701048` and `51701312` were cancelled only to replace their execution with the registered parallel audit chain. The first replacement submission was rejected before any job was accepted because Slurm no longer retained completed affine array `51701047` as a dependency. All nine affine `completed.json` records were verified PASS (21 records each and matching config SHA256); the accepted Han audit still depends on active neural array `51701046`. No training or prediction job was cancelled, and no result was selected or discarded. Actual start, node and completion events will be preserved in `job_events.jsonl` and the final registry entry.

## 2026-09-10 Task6/7/8 resource balance and stalled evaluator recovery

- At 15:13:50 +03, original array `51701046` concurrency changed from 27 to 18 to leave room for the later directional array. At 15:36:16 it returned to 27 after the original batch had mostly completed; directional array `51701310` increased from 9 to 18. These changes affect concurrency only. Slurm returned nonzero for some already-finished array children; active-array limits were independently verified and recorded in `resource_adjustments.jsonl`.
- Original `51701046_22` (actual job `51701871`, Boeing 747 / Raw / seed9110) trained both networks, then remained CPU-active for over an hour while writing only 2 of 30 evaluation records. Nonblocking `py-spy 0.4.1` stack sampling identified `HanFlowMapData_3D.py:174`, the original all-pairs distance evaluation. Only stacks and the integer counters n=276, q=128, times=125, chunk=8, i=168 were retained; no model variables or weights were saved. The profiler was installed only under the report checkout’s temporary directory. Read-only `ps`, tool-availability checks and sampling ran as steps of the existing allocation.
- Recovery commit `fddec88b91f93ca430f9ae3edd97152a01a6b9c4` verifies eight training, encoder and predictor source files are identical to frozen commit `45eefee828f5439f6aad6940b01aafe7803aa691` (line endings normalized for the source check). Only the evaluation function reference is replaced by the already validated equivalent Euclidean-distance kernel. No data, model, seed, optimizer budget, metric definition or particle pair is changed.
- On the SAME node and SAME saved predictions, the new kernel reproduced all original Task6/Task7 fit metrics with maximum absolute differences 4.12e-18 and 2.17e-19. Task6/7/8 full fit evaluations took 2.810/1.400/2.970 seconds. The stalled original Task8 had not emitted its metric, so the Task8 record is a timing/finite-result check rather than a direct comparison to a completed original Task8 result. Full independent replay remains mandatory. Evidence: `outputs/Verify_Task678_HanSampling_1.1/runtime_recovery_checks/boeing747_raw_positions_seed9110.json`, SHA256 `95f9c5fcb0939c74d372e07f1acba78fb831fae8ec7eec93a0bbd4e6d3c6d123`.
- Recovery job **51707303** was submitted held at **15:53:11 +03**, with the same A100/V100 constraint, 4 CPU cores, 48 GB and 2-hour limit. Config SHA256 remains `dd58fefe440bc75c5eaa49d1d017a2ccb04ed457977226da167d04da15247ce9`. Its code and command are in `runtime_recovery_submissions.jsonl`.
- Before cancelling the original process, audit array `51703005` was changed to the AND dependency `afterany:51701046,afterok:51707303`. It still checks every canonical completion record and all 2376 metrics; the dependency change permits the deliberately cancelled execution attempt while requiring successful recovery. Collector and fixed-example dependencies remain intact.
- Cancellation was requested at **15:54:18 +03**; after the process ended, all 10 original output files were moved intact to `failed_attempts/stalled_eval_51701046_22`, with a SHA256 recorded for every file. Recovery was released at **15:54:27 +03**. No completed test result was discarded, and the interrupted attempt is retained as an execution failure, not an extra seed. Metadata: `runtime_recovery_dependency.json`, `runtime_recovery_state.json`; stack and counter evidence: `outputs/Task678_HanAndVector_1.1/diagnostics/`.
- This is an execution recovery, not a new feature-method candidate or a change selected from test performance. Canonical audit provenance will therefore list both execution commits; the eight frozen algorithm-source hashes establish that the compared method is unchanged. Actual recovery node, GPU, start/end and final status will be included in the completion record.


## 2026-09-10 Task6/7/8 HanSampling + VectorFMT completion register

Both frozen experiments completed: HanSampling 81 canonical neural shards + 9 deterministic shards, 2376 replayed evaluation records; VectorFMT 27 neural + 9 deterministic shards, 999 records. Each neural shard trains Task6 and Task7; Task8 reuses Task6. All nine per-flow audits and both collectors passed. Method-level conclusions, including failed generalization, are recorded only in `docs/experiment_log.md#task678-han-vector-final-20260910`.

- Han final collector 51703006: program start/end 16:12:12.585/16:12:19.458 +03, cn604-18, CPU, audit commit ea1054f9af40e68ab318c7e96d02406c535f56d9. Vector collector 51703008: 15:58:45.866/15:58:53.123 +03, cn604-18, CPU, same audit commit. These are auditing commits, not the training commits.
- Recovery 51707303 completed on gpu210-14, Tesla V100-SXM2-32GB; scheduler 15:54:37–16:04:01 +03 (9m24s), actual predictor metadata begins 15:54:44.346. Frozen training/config/seed verified; Task6/7/8 fit prediction archives match the interrupted run byte for byte. `runtime_recovery_prediction_comparison.json` records SHA256 2779ad1a263b708ed83a807dc0e94f44fc691e3722b872e7dd26ce5d0a2d66ce / 0beca9b66e2a58f503239afa507c0f4a12a642ad38c6cd79facba7055c87b663 / 2365e2324fd351525b295937c9babc2724f6e5292da28c4aac1266ba7b981f55. Original attempt and all 10 files remain preserved.
- Two generic wrapper fields are inaccurate and are preserved rather than silently rewritten: the recovery phase was labeled CPU in job_events, but its completed.json and scheduler allocation identify the V100; the canceled original 51701046_22 wrapper emitted COMPLETED/0 after cancellation, but Slurm 51701871 is CANCELLED at 15:54:18 and it lacked a canonical completed.json. The table uses scheduler cancellation and actual predictor device. The accepted replacement alone contributes the scientific seed.
- Quota-failed build 51700832_7 had no ending JSONL event; an explicit JobID/JobIDRaw scheduler check maps it to 51700972, FAILED at 14:01:20 on cn511-19. The table uses that scheduler end state and preserves the missing-event record.
- Fixed examples 51704726 completed 16:14:33.078–16:14:37.915 +03 on cn604-17, CPU, commit b8d3a53bfbed1c7251af33f0860c2d6db3ba8817. All nine first-index examples and source hashes exported after both audits passed; local rendering changes no predictions.
- Audited metadata delivery: Han archive SHA256 86dd3b8be6b5ca4aa55490f1d42b8ee1a6cab6ccbc8f0a1f12fb5d37607a449a, 2797 verified files; Vector archive 82115d4457503da09646e31b5b98dfa3df162091c749f017f24c6879d3bfb94a, 1161 verified files. Full event JSONL, Slurm accounting, submission records, configs, seeds, source hashes and metrics are in each local evidence_metadata directory. No checkpoint files saved or downloaded. Only nine small fixed-example NPZ arrays were downloaded for these figures.
- Home-quota failures, all pretraining cancellations, replaced serial audits and the interrupted evaluation remain in this register and the immutable source metadata. No failed test case, seed or dataset was removed to improve metrics.
